Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The geochemistry operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 523. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 384 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 523 + O(1/n^384). Summing over all modes and applying the geochemistry convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
